const {\n    return (pieces(color, Knight) | pieces(color, Bishop) | pieces(color, Rook) | pieces(color, Queen)) != 0;\n}\n\nbool Board::see_ge(const Move& move, int threshold) const {\n    if (move.is_castle()) return 0 >= threshold;\n    const Color us = side_;\n    const int from = move.from;\n    const int to = move.to;\n    const int captured_square = (move.flags & EnPassant) != 0U ? to - pawn_push_delta(us) : to;\n    const int target_rank = rank_of(to);\n    if ((target_rank == 0 || target_rank == 7) &&\n        piece_type(squares_[static_cast<std::size_t>(from)]) != King) {\n        const Color promoter = target_rank == 7 ? White : Black;\n        const Bitboard promoting_pawns =\n            pieces(promoter, Pawn) & attacks::pawn(opposite(promoter), to) &\n            ~(square_bb(from) | square_bb(captured_square));\n        if (promoting_pawns != 0) {\n            return promotion_recapture_exchange(*this, move, captured_square) >= threshold;\n        }\n    }\n    int swap = SeeValue[static_cast<std::size_t>(piece_type(squares_[static_cast<std::size_t>(captured_square)]))] - threshold;\n    int attacker_value = SeeValue[static_cast<std::size_t>(piece_type(squares_[static_cast<std::size_t>(from)]))];\n    if (move.is_promotion()) {\n        swap += SeeValue[move.promotion] - SeeValue[Pawn];\n        attacker_value = SeeValue[move.promotion];\n    }\n    if (swap < 0) return false;\n    swap = attacker_value - swap;\n    if (swap <= 0) return true;\n\n    Bitboard occupancy = (occupied() ^ square_bb(from) ^ square_bb(captured_square)) | square_bb(to);\n    Color stm = us;\n    Bitboard attackers = attackers_to_fast(*this, to, occupancy);\n    const Bitboard diagonals = pieces(Bishop) | pieces(Queen);\n    const Bitboard lines = pieces(Rook) | pieces(Queen);\n    int result = 1;\n#if FORGE_SDK_SEE_HOIST\n    // Per side, once per call and only when first needed: the enemy pieces that touch its king (pawn, knight,\n    // king contact) and the enemy sliders on its king's empty-board lines. The pieces do not move during the\n    // exchange; only `live` loses bits, so `set & live` is exactly what the loop used to rebuild at every step.\n    std::array<Bitboard, 2> contact{};\n    std::array<Bitboard, 2> snipers{};\n    unsigned prepared = 0;\n#endif\n    while (true) {\n        stm = opposite(stm);\n        attackers &= occupancy;\n        Bitboard stm_attackers = attackers & occupied(stm);\n        // Every candidate only removes its own origin; the target stays occupied.\n        // Existing checks reject every non-king; singleton ray blockers identify\n        // exactly the origins whose departure would expose a live enemy slider.\n        const Bitboard king_attackers = stm_attackers & pieces(stm, King);\n#if FORGE_SDK_SEE_HOIST\n        if ((stm_attackers & ~king_attackers) != 0) {\n            const std::size_t side = static_cast<std::size_t>(stm);\n            const int king = king_square(stm);\n            if ((prepared & (1U << side)) == 0U) {\n                const Color enemy = opposite(stm);\n                const Bitboard queens = pieces(enemy, Queen);\n                contact[side] = (attacks::pawn(stm, king) & pieces(enemy, Pawn)) |\n                                (attacks::knight(king) & pieces(enemy, Knight)) |\n                                (attacks::king(king) & pieces(enemy, King));\n                snipers[side] = (attacks::bishop_ray(king) & (pieces(enemy, Bishop) | queens)) |\n                                (attacks::rook_ray(king) & (pieces(enemy, Rook) | queens));\n                prepared |= 1U << side;\n            }\n            const Bitboard live = occupancy & ~square_bb(to);\n            bool checked = (contact[side] & live) != 0;\n            Bitboard pinners = snipers[side] & live;\n            // Neither a contact check nor a live slider on a king line: nothing to filter (the common case).\n            if (checked || pinners != 0) {\n                Bitboard forbidden = 0;\n                while (!checked && pinners != 0) {\n                    const int pinner = pop_lsb(pinners);\n                    const Bitboard blockers = attacks::between(king, pinner) & occupancy;\n                    if (blockers == 0) checked = true;\n                    else if (!more_than_one(blockers)) forbidden |= blockers;\n                }\n                stm_attackers = checked ? king_attackers : stm_attackers & ~forbidden;\n            }\n        }\n#else\n        if ((stm_attackers & ~king_attackers) != 0) {\n            const Color enemy = opposite(stm);\n            const int king = king_square(stm);\n            const Bitboard live = occupancy & ~square_bb(to);\n            const Bitboard queens = pieces(enemy, Queen) & live;\n            bool checked =\n                (attacks::pawn(stm, king) & pieces(enemy, Pawn) & live) != 0 ||\n                (attacks::knight(king) & pieces(enemy, Knight) & live) != 0 ||\n                (attacks::king(king) & pieces(enemy, King) & live) != 0;\n            Bitboard pinners =\n                (attacks::bishop_ray(king) & ((pieces(enemy, Bishop) & live) | queens)) |\n                (attacks::rook_ray(king) & ((pieces(enemy, Rook) & live) | queens));\n            Bitboard forbidden = 0;\n            while (!checked && pinners != 0) {\n                const int pinner = pop_lsb(pinners);\n                const Bitboard blockers = attacks::between(king, pinner) & occupancy;\n                if (blockers == 0) checked = true;\n                else if (!more_than_one(blockers)) forbidden |= blockers;\n            }\n            stm_attackers = checked ? king_attackers : stm_attackers & ~forbidden;\n        }\n#endif\n        if (stm_attackers == 0) break;\n        result ^= 1;\n        Bitboard bb = 0;\n        if ((bb = stm_attackers & pieces(stm, Pawn)) != 0) {\n            if ((swap = SeeValue[Pawn] - swap) < result) break;\n            occupancy ^= square_bb(lsb(bb));\n            attackers |= attacks::bishop(to, occupancy) & diagonals;\n        } else if ((bb = stm_attackers & pieces(stm, Knight)) != 0) {\n            if ((swap = SeeValue[Knight] - swap) < result) break;\n            occupancy ^= square_bb(lsb(bb));\n        } else if ((bb = stm_attackers & pieces(stm, Bishop)) != 0) {\n            if ((swap = SeeValue[Bishop] - swap) < result) break;\n            occupancy ^= square_bb(lsb(bb));\n            attackers |= attacks::bishop(to, occupancy) & diagonals;\n        } else if ((bb = stm_attackers & pieces(stm, Rook)) != 0) {\n            if ((swap = SeeValue[Rook] - Quiets emits the pushes,\n// then the same piece loops over the empty squares only, then castling: the\n// subsequence of All that the move picker's filter keeps, in the same order.\n// Mode is a compile-time constant so the capture/quiet branches fold away.\ntemplate <Board::GenMode Mode>\nvoid fill_pseudo_legal(const Board& board, MoveList& moves) {\n    constexpr bool captures_only = Mode == Board::GenMode::Captures;\n    constexpr bool quiets_only = Mode == Board::GenMode::Quiets;\n    const Color us = board.side_to_move();\n    const Color them = opposite(us);\n    const Bitboard own = board.occupied(us);\n    const Bitboard enemy = board.occupied(them);\n    const Bitboard occ = own | enemy;\n    const Bitboard empty = ~occ;\n    const int up = pawn_push_delta(us);\n\n    auto push = [&](int from, int to, std::uint8_t flags) {\n        moves.push_back(Move{static_cast<std::uint8_t>(from), static_cast<std::uint8_t>(to), 0, flags});\n    };\n    auto push_promotions = [&](int from, int to, std::uint8_t flags) {\n        moves.push_back(Move{static_cast<std::uint8_t>(from), static_cast<std::uint8_t>(to), Queen,\n                             static_cast<std::uint8_t>(flags | Promotion)});\n        for (const int promotion : {Rook, Bishop, Knight}) {\n            moves.push_back(Move{static_cast<std::uint8_t>(from), static_cast<std::uint8_t>(to), static_cast<std::uint8_t>(promotion),\n                                 static_cast<std::uint8_t>(flags | Promotion)});\n        }\n    };\n\n    const Bitboard pawns = board.pieces(us, Pawn);\n    const Bitboard seventh = us == White ? Rank7BB : Rank2BB;\n    const Bitboard third = us == White ? Rank3BB : Rank6BB;\n    const Bitboard promoting = pawns & seventh;\n    const Bitboard regular = pawns & ~seventh;\n\n    if constexpr (!captures_only) {\n        Bitboard single = pawn_push(us, regular) & empty;\n        Bitboard twice = pawn_push(us, single & third) & empty;\n        while (single != 0) {\n            const int to = pop_lsb(single);\n            push(to - up, to, Quiet);\n        }\n        while (twice != 0) {\n            const int to = pop_lsb(twice);\n            push(to - 2 * up, to, DoublePush);\n        }\n    }\n    if constexpr (!quiets_only) {\n        Bitboard promo_push = pawn_push(us, promoting) & empty;\n        while (promo_push != 0) {\n            const int to = pop_lsb(promo_push);\n            push_promotions(to - up, to, Quiet);\n        }\n        Bitboard capturers = pawns;\n        while (capturers != 0) {\n            const int from = pop_lsb(capturers);\n            Bitboard targets = attacks::pawn(us, from) & enemy;\n            const bool promotes = (square_bb(from) & seventh) != 0;\n            while (targets != 0) {\n                const int to = pop_lsb(targets);\n                if (promotes) push_promotions(from, to, Capture);\n                else push(from, to, Capture);\n            }\n        }\n        if (board.ep_square() >= 0) {\n            const int ep_square = board.ep_square();\n            Bitboard ep_attackers = attacks::pawn(them, ep_square) & pawns;\n            while (ep_attackers != 0) {\n                push(pop_lsb(ep_attackers), ep_square, static_cast<std::uint8_t>(Capture | EnPassant));\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    const Bitboard targets_mask = captures_only ? enemy : (quiets_only ? empty : ~own);\n    auto emit = [&](int from, Bitboard targets) {\n        while (targets != 0) {\n            const int to = pop_lsb(targets);\n            std::uint8_t flags = Quiet;\n            if constexpr (captures_only) flags = Capture;\n            else if constexpr (!quiets_only) flags = (square_bb(to) & enemy) != 0 ? Capture : Quiet;\n            push(from, to, flags);\n        }\n    };\n    Bitboard knights = board.pieces(us, Knight);\n    while (knights != 0) {\n        const int from = pop_lsb(knights);\n        emit(from, attacks::knight(from) & targets_mask);\n    }\n    Bitboard bishops = board.pieces(us, Bishop);\n    while (bishops != 0) {\n        const int from = pop_lsb(bishops);\n        emit(from, attacks::bishop(from, occ) & targets_mask);\n    }\n    Bitboard rooks = board.pieces(us, Rook);\n    while (rooks != 0) {\n        const int from = pop_lsb(rooks);\n        emit(from, attacks::rook(from, occ) & targets_mask);\n    }\n    Bitboard queens = board.pieces(us, Queen);\n    while (queens != 0) {\n        const int from = pop_lsb(queens);\n        emit(from, attacks::queen(from, occ) & targets_mask);\n    }\n    const int king = board.king_square(us);\n    emit(king, attacks::king(king) & targets_mask);\n\n    if constexpr (!captures_only) {\n        if (board.checkers() == 0) {\n            const std::uint8_t rights = board.castling_rights();\n            if (us == White && king == 4) {\n                if ((rights & WhiteKingSide) != 0U && (occ & (square_bb(5) | square_bb(6))) == 0 &&\n                    board.piece_at(7) == WR && !board.square_attacked(5, Black) && !board.square_attacked(6, Black)) {\n                    push(4, 6, KingCastle);\n                }\n                if ((rights & WhiteQueenSide) != 0U &&\n                    (occ & (square_bb(1) | square_bb(2) | square_bb(3))) == 0 &&\n                    board.piece_at(0) == WR && !board.square_attacked(3, Black) && !board.square_attacked(2, Black)) {\n                    push(4, 2, QueenCastle);\n                }\n            } else if (us == Black && king == 60) {\n                if ((rights & BlackKingSide) != 0U && (occ & (square_bb(61) | square_bb(62))) == 0 &&\n                    board.piece_at(63) == BR && !board.square_attacked(61, White) && !board.square_attacked(62, White)) {\n                    push(60, 62, KingCastle);\n                }\n                if ((rights & BlackQueenSide) != 0U &&\n                    (occ & (square_bb(57) | square_bb(58) | square_bb(59))) == 0 &&\n                    board.piece_at(56) == BR && !board.square_attacked(59, White) && !board.square_attacked(58, White)) {\n                    push(60, 58, QueenCastle);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid Board::generate_pseudo_legal(MoveList& moves, GenMode mode) const {\n    switch (mode) {\n    case GenMode::Captures: fill_pseudo_legal<GenMode::Captures>(*this, moves); break;\n    case GenMode::Quiets: fill_pseudo_legal<GenMode::Quiets>(*this, moves); break;\n    case GenMode::All: fill_pseudo_legal<GenMode::All>(*this, moves); break;\n    }\n}\n#else\nvoid Board::generate_pseudo_legal(MoveList& moves, bool captures_only) const {\n    const Color us = side_;\n    const Color them = opposite(us);\n    const Bitboard own = occupied(us);\n    const Bitboard enemy = occupied(them);\n    const Bitboard occ = own | enemy;\n    const Bitboard empty = ~occ;\n    const int up = pawn_push_delta(us);\n\n    auto push = [&](int from, int to, std::uint8_t flags) {\n        moves.push_back(Move{static_cast<std::uint8_t>(from), static_cast<std::uint8_t>(to), 0, flags});\n    };\n    auto push_promotions = [&](int from, int to, std::uint8_t flags) {\n        moves.push_back(Move{static_cast<std::uint8_t>(from), static_cast<std::uint8_t>(to), Queen,\n                             static_cast<std::uint8_t>(flags | Promotion)});\n        for (const int promotion : {Rook, Bishop, Knight}) {\n            moves.push_back(Move{static_cast<std::uint8_t>(from), static_cast<std::uint8_t>(to), static_cast<std::uint8_t>(promotion),\n                                 static_cast<std::uint8_t>(flags | Promotion)});\n        }\n    };\n\n    const Bitboard pawns = Capture : Quiet);\n        }\n    };\n    Bitboard knights = pieces(us, Knight);\n    while (knights != 0) {\n        const int from = pop_lsb(knights);\n        emit(from, attacks::knight(from) & targets_mask);\n    }\n    Bitboard bishops = pieces(us, Bishop);\n    while (bishops != 0) {\n        const int from = pop_lsb(bishops);\n        emit(from, attacks::bishop(from, occ) & targets_mask);\n    }\n    Bitboard rooks = pieces(us, Rook);\n    while (rooks != 0) {\n        const int from = pop_lsb(rooks);\n        emit(from, attacks::rook(from, occ) & targets_mask);\n    }\n    Bitboard queens = pieces(us, Queen);\n    while (queens != 0) {\n        const int from = pop_lsb(queens);\n        emit(from, attacks::queen(from, occ) & targets_mask);\n    }\n    const int king = king_square(us);\n    emit(king, attacks::king(king) & targets_mask);\n\n    if (!captures_only && checkers_ == 0) {\n        if (us == White && king == 4) {\n            if ((castling_ & captures_only) const {\n    MoveList moves;\n    generate_pseudo_legal(moves, captures_only);\n    return moves;\n}\n\nMoveList Board::legal_moves(bool captures_only) const {\n    MoveList moves = pseudo_legal_moves(captures_only);\n    std::size_t write = 0;\n    for (const Move move : moves) {\n        if (is_legal(move)) moves[write++] = move;\n    }\n    moves.resize(write);\n    return moves;\n}\n\nbool Board::has_legal_move() const {\n    // A single legal quiet pawn push proves non-terminal without generating\n    // the full move list. Promotions and every failed witness use the original\n    // complete fallback; exact king-safety testing handles checks and pins.\n    const Bitboard pushes = pawn_push(side_, pieces(side_, Pawn)) & ~occupied() & ~(Rank1BB | Rank8BB);\n    if (pushes != 0) {\n        const int to = lsb(pushes);\n        const Move witness{static_cast<std::uint8_t>(to - pawn_push_delta(side_)),\n                           static_cast<std::uint8_t>(to), 0, Quiet};\n        if (is_legal(witness)) return true;\n    }\n    MoveList moves;\n    generate_pseudo_legal(moves, false);\n    for (const Move move : moves) {\n        if (is_legal(move)) return true;\n    }\n    return false;\n}\n\n#if FORGE_SPD_KING_LINES\nbool Board::is_legal_complete(const Move& move) const {\n    const Color us = side_;\n    const Piece moving = squares_[move.from];\n    // Not in check, not en passant, not a king move: the own king can only\n    // become attacked when a pinned piece leaves the line to its pinner. A\n    // capture keeps its target square occupied, so it never opens a line.\n    if (checkers_ == 0 && (move.flags & EnPassant) == 0U && piece_type(moving) != King) {\n        if (!king_lines_.valid) refresh_king_lines();\n        return (king_lines_.pinned & square_bb(move.from)) == 0 ||\n               attacks::aligned(king_square(us), move.from, move.to);\n    }\n    Bitboard occupancy = occupied() & ~square_bb(move.from);\n    int captured_square = move.to;\n    if ((move.flags & EnPassant) != 0U) captured_square = move.to - pawn_push_delta(us);\n    Bitboard removed = 0;\n    if (move.is_capture()) {\n        removed = square_bb(captured_square);\n        occupancy &= ~removed;\n    }\n    occupancy |= square_bb(move.to);\n    if (move.is_castle()) {\n        const bool king_side = (move.flags & KingCastle) != 0U;\n        const int rook_from = us == White ? (king_side ? 7 : 0) : (king_side ? 63 : 56);\n        const int rook_to = us == White ?   if (move.from >= 64 || move.to >= 64 || move.from == move.to) return false;\n    const Color us = side_;\n    const Piece moving = squares_[move.from];\n    if (moving == Empty || color_of(moving) != us) return false;\n    const Piece target = squares_[move.to];\n    if (target != Empty && (color_of(target) == us || piece_type(target) == King)) return false;\n    const int type = piece_type(moving);\n    const Bitboard occ = occupied();\n    const int up = pawn_push_delta(us);\n    const std::uint8_t special = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(move.flags & (DoublePush | KingCastle | QueenCastle | EnPassant | Promotion));\n\n    if (move.is_castle()) {\n        if (type != King || checkers_ != 0 || special != (move.flags & (KingCastle | QueenCastle))) return false;\n        if ((move.flags & Capture) != 0U || move.promotion != 0) return false;\n        const bool king_side = (move.flags & KingCastle) != 0U;\n        if (king_side && (move.flags & QueenCastle) != 0U) return == 0 &&\n                   piece_at(63) == BR && !square_attacked(61, White) && !square_attacked(62, White);\n        }\n        return (castling_ & BlackQueenSide) != 0U && (occ & (square_bb(57) | square_bb(58) | square_bb(59))) == 0 &&\n               piece_at(56) == BR && !square_attacked(59, White) && !square_attacked(58, White);\n    }\n\n    if (type == Pawn) {\n        const bool promotes = relative_rank(us, move.from) == 6;\n        if (promotes != move.is_promotion()) return false;\n        if (move.is_promotion() && (move.promotion < Knight || move.promotion > Queen)) return false;\n        if (!move.is_promotion() && move.promotion != 0) return false;\n        if ((move.flags & EnPassant) != 0U) {\n            if (ep_square_ < 0 || move.to != ep_square_ || (move.flags & Capture) == 0U) return false;\n            if ((move.flags & (DoublePush | Promotion)) != 0U) return false;\n            return (attacks::pawn(us, move.from) & square_bb(move.to)) != 0;\n        }\n        if ((move.flags & Capture) != 0U) {\n            if (target == Empty || (move.flags & DoublePush) != 0U) return false;\n            return (attacks::pawn(us, move.from) & square_bb(move.to)) != 0;\n        }\n        if (target != Empty) return false;\n        if ((move.flags & DoublePush) != 0U) {\n            if (relative_rank(us, move.from) != 1 || move.to != move.from + 2 * up) return false;\n            return squares_[static_cast<std::size_t>(move.from + up)] == Empty;\n        }\n        return move.to == move.from + up;\n    }\n\n    if (special != 0 || move.promotion != 0) King: reach = attacks::king(move.from); break;\n    default: return false;\n    }\n    return (reach & square_bb(move.to)) != 0;\n}\n\n#if FORGE_SPD_KING_LINES\nbool Board::gives_check_complete(const Move& move) const {\n    const Color us = side_;\n    const Color them = opposite(us);\n    const int ksq = king_square(them);\n    // Normal moves: a direct check comes from a square that already sees the\n    // enemy king (the vacated origin lies on that line only when the mover\n    // checked the king before the move, which a legal position excludes); a\n    // discovered check comes from a sole blocker leaving its line.\n    if ((move.flags & (EnPassant | KingCastle | QueenCastle | Promotion)) == 0U) {\n        if (!king_lines_.valid) refresh_king_lines();\n        const int type = piece_type(squares_[move.from]);\n        if ((king_lines_.check_squares[static_cast<std::size_t>(type)] & square_bb(move.to)) != 0) return true;\n        return (king_lines_.discoverers & square_bb(move.from)) != 0 && !attacks::aligned(ksq, move.from, move.to);\n    }\n    const Bitboard king_bit = square_bb(ksq);\n    Bitboard occ = (occupied() ^ square_bb(move.from)) | square_bb(move.to);\n    if ((move.flags & EnPassant) != 0U) occ &= ~square_bb(move.to - pawn_push_delta(us));\n    if (move.is_castle()) {\n        const bool king_side = (move.flags & KingCastle) != 0U;\n        const int rook_from = us == White ? (king_side ? 7 : 0) : (king_side ? 63 : 56);\n        const int rook_to = us == White ? (king_side ? 5 : 3) : (king_side ? 61 : 59);\n        occ = (occ & ~square_bb(rook_from)) | square_bb(rook_to);\n        if ((attacks::rook(rook_to, occ) & king_bit) != 0) return true;\n    }\n    const int type = move.is_promotion() ? move.promotion : piece_type(squares_[move.from]);\n    Bitboard direct = 0;\n    switch (type) {\n    case Pawn: direct = attacks::pawn(us, move.to); break;\n    case Knight: direct = attacks::knight(move.to); break;\n    case Bishop: direct = attacks::bishop(move.to, occ); break;\n    case Rook: direct = attacks::rook(move.to, occ); break;\n    case Queen: direct = attacks::queen(move.to, occ); break;\n    default: break;\n    }\n    if ((direct & king_bit) != 0) return true;\n    const Bitboard moved = square_bb(move.from);\n    const Bitboard diagonals = (pieces(us, Bishop) | pieces(us, Queen)) & ~moved;\n    const Bitboard lines = (pieces(us, Rook) | pieces(us, Queen)) & ~moved;\n    if (diagonals != 0 && (attacks::bishop(ksq, occ) & diagonals) != 0) return true;\n    return lines != 0 && (attacks::rook(ksq, occ) & lines) != 0;\n}\n\nnamespace {\n// Pieces of either colour that alone stand between `king` and one of the\n// given rook-like / bishop-like sliders (the lock's see_ge uses the same walk\n// over the empty-board rays and the between masks).\n[[nodiscard]] Bitboard single_blockers(int king, Bitboard rook_like, Bitboard bishop_like, Bitboard occupancy) {\n    Bitboard snipers = (attacks::rook_ray(king) & rook_like) | (attacks::bishop_ray(king) & bishop_like);\n    Bitboard blockers = 0;\n    while (snipers != 0) {\n        const Bitboard between = attacks::between(king, pop_lsb(snipers)) & occupancy;\n        if (between != 0 && !more_than_one(between)) blockers |= between;\n    }\n    return blockers;\n}\n} // namespace\n\nvoid Board::refresh_king_lines() const {\n    const Color us = side_;\n    const Color them = opposite(us);\n    const Bitboard occupancy = occupied();\n    const int our_king = king_square(us);\n    const int their_king = king_square(them);\n    KingLines& lines = king_lines_;\n    lines.pinned = single_blockers(our_king, pieces(them, Rook) | pieces(them, Queen),\n                                   pieces(them, Bishop) | pieces(them, Queen), occupancy) & occupied(us);\n    lines.discoverers = single_blockers(their_king, pieces(us, Rook) | pieces(us, Queen),\n                                        pieces(us, Bishop) | pieces(us, Queen), occupancy) & occupied(us);\n    lines.check_squares[NoPieceType] = 0;\n    lines.check_squares[Pawn] = attacks::pawn(them, their_king);\n    lines.check_squares[Knight] = attacks::knight(their_king);\n    lines.check_squares[Bishop] = attacks::bishop(their_king, occupancy);\n    lines.check_squares[Rook] = attacks::rook(their_king, occupancy);\n    lines.check_squares[Queen] = lines.check_squares[Bishop] | lines.check_squares[Rook];\n    lines.check_squares[King] = 0;\n    lines.valid = true;\n}\n#else\nbool Board::gives_check_complete(const Move& move) const {\n    const Color us = side_;\n    const Color them = opposite(us);\n    const int ksq = king_square(them);\n    const Bitboard king_bit = square_bb(ksq);\n    Bitboard occ = (occupied() ^ square_bb(move.from)) | square_bb(move.to);\n    if ((move.flags & EnPassant) != 0U) occ &= ~square_bb(move.to - pawn_push_delta(us));\n    if (move.is_castle()) {\n        const bool king_side = (move.flags & KingCastle) != 0U;\n        const int rook_from = us == White ? }\n[[nodiscard]] int mate_in(int ply) { return MateValue - ply; }\n\nstruct ReductionTable {\n    std::array<std::array<std::uint8_t, 64>, 64> quiet{};\n    std::array<std::array<std::uint8_t, 64>, 64> noisy{};\n    ReductionTable() { refresh(); }\n    void refresh() {\n        for (int depth = 1; depth < 64; ++depth) {\n            for (int moves = 1; moves < 64; ++moves) {\n                const double product = std::log(static_cast<double>(depth)) * std::log(static_cast<double>(moves));\n                quiet[static_cast<std::size_t>(depth)][static_cast<std::size_t>(moves)] = Reductions.quiet[d][m] : Reductions.noisy[d][m];\n}\n\n[[nodiscard]] int lmp_threshold(int depth, bool improving) {\n    return improving ? tune::LmpBase + depth * depth : (tune::LmpBase + depth * std::abs(clamped) / HistoryMax);\n}\n\n[[nodiscard]] int capture_value(const Board& board, Move move) {\n    int value = half weight), summed in this order (= Worker::quiet_history).\n[[nodiscard]] inline int quiet_history_sum(const std::array<std::array<std::int16_t, 64>, 64>& main,\n                                           const PieceToHistory* cont1, const PieceToHistory* cont2,\n                                           const PieceToHistory* cont4, Piece piece, Move move) {\n    const std::size_t p = static_cast<std::size_t>(piece);\n    int score = main[move.from][move.to];\n    if (cont1 != nullptr) score += (*cont1)[p][move.to];\n    if (cont2 != nullptr) score += (*cont2)[p][move.to];\n    if namespace\n\nvoid tune::refresh() { Reductions.refresh(); }\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Transposition table\n\nthread_local const TranspositionTable::ExclusiveLease* TranspositionTable::active_lease_ = nullptr;\n\nTranspositionTable::ExclusiveLease::ExclusiveLease(TranspositionTable& table)\n    : table_(table), previous_(active_lease_), gate_(table.exclusive_gate_, std::defer_lock) {\n    if (!table_.has_exclusive_access()) {\n        gate_.lock();\n        table_.exclusive_requested_.store(true, std::memory_order_release);\n        try {\n            // A foreign accessor either completes before its stripe drains,\n            // or observes the request after acquiring that drained stripe.\n            for (std::size_t i = 0; i < StripeCount; ++i) {\n                const std::lock_guard<std::mutex> drain(table_.stripes_[i].mutex);\n            }\n        } catch (...) {\n            table_.exclusive_requested_.store(false, std::memory_order_release);\n            throw;\n        }\n    }\n    active_lease_ = this;\n}\n\nTranspositionTable::ExclusiveLease::~ExclusiveLease() {\n    // Publish completed writes before member destruction releases the gate.\n    active_lease_ = previous_;\n    if (gate_.owns_lock()) table_.exclusive_requested_.store(false, std::memory_order_release);\n}\n\nbool TranspositionTable::has_exclusive_access() const {\n    for (const ExclusiveLease* lease = active_lease_; lease != nullptr; lease = lease->previous_) {\n        if (&lease->table_ == this) return true;\n    }\n    return false;\n}\n\nstd::unique_lock<std::mutex> TranspositionTable::lock_stripe(std::size_t index) const {\n    std::mutex& stripe = stripes_[index % StripeCount].mutex;\n    if (has_exclusive_access()) return std::unique_lock<std::mutex>(stripe, std::defer_lock);\n    for (;;) {\n        std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(stripe);\n        if (!exclusive_requested_.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) return lock;\n        // Never wait for the gate while holding a stripe: the owner drains it.\n        lock.unlock();\n        const std::lock_guard<std::mutex> wait(exclusive_gate_);\n    }\n}\n\nvoid TranspositionTable::resize(std::size_t mb) {\n    const std::size_t bytes = std::max<std::size_t>(1, TTCluster& cluster = cluster_for(key);\n    const std::size_t index = static_cast<std::size_t>(&cluster - clusters_.get());\n    // Owner calls (TakeLock false) skip lock_stripe: under the lease it only hands\n    // back a deferred (unlocked) lock, after a thread_local lookup per call.\n    const auto lock = TakeLock ? lock_stripe(index) : std::unique_lock<std::mutex>();\n    auto snapshot = [&](const TTEntry& entry, bool hit) {\n        TTProbe result;\n        result.entry = entry;\n        result.hit = hit;\n        result.owner_ = this;\n        result.cluster_ = index;\n        result.slot_ = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(&entry - cluster.entries.data());\n        result.epoch_ = epoch_;\n        result.key_ = key;\n        return result;\n    };\n    const std::uint32_t key32 = verification_key(key);\n    for (TTEntry& entry : cluster.entries) {\n        if (entry.key32 == key32 && entry.occupied()) {\n            // Reused entries remain recent even when search returns a TT cutoff.\n            if (entry.generation() != generation_) {\n                entry.gen_bound = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(\n                    (static_cast<unsigned>(generation_) << 3U) | (entry.gen_bound & 7U));\n            }\n            return snapshot(entry, true);\n        }\n    }\n    const TTEntry* replace = &cluster.entries[0];\n    auto relative_age = [this](const TTEntry& snapshot(entry, false);\n        if (entry.depth - 4 * relative_age(entry) < replace->depth - 4 * relative_age(*replace)) replace = &entry;\n    }\n    return snapshot(*replace, false);\n}\n\nTTProbe return probe_body<false>(key);\n}\n\ntemplate <bool TakeLock>\nvoid TranspositionTable::store_body(const TTProbe& probe, std::uint64_t key, int score, int eval, Move move, int depth,\n                                    Bound bound, bool pv, std::uint16_t halfmove_clock) {\n    // Reject default, foreign or lifecycle-stale handles without touching storage.\n    // Lifecycle operations themselves must not run concurrently with search.\n    if (probe.owner_ != this || probe.epoch_ != epoch_ || probe.key_ != key ||\n        probe.cluster_ >= cluster_count_ || probe.slot_ >= 4) return;\n    const TranspositionTable::store(const TTProbe& probe, std::uint64_t key, int score, int eval, Move move, int depth,\n                               Bound bound, bool pv, std::uint16_t halfmove_clock) {\n    // Reject default, foreign or lifecycle-stale handles without touching storage.\n    // Lifecycle operations themselves must not run concurrently with search.\n    if (probe.owner_ != this || probe.epoch_ != epoch_ || probe.key_ != key ||\n        probe.cluster_ >= cluster_count_ || probe.slot_ >= 4) return;\n    const auto lock = lock_stripe(probe.cluster_);\n    TTEntry* entry = &clusters_[probe.cluster_].entries[probe.slot_];\n    const std::uint32_t key32 = verification_key(key);\n    const bool same_position = entry->key32 == key32 && entry->occupied();\n    if (!move.is_none() || !same_position) entry->move = move;\n    if (bound == BoundExact || !same_position || depth + 4 > entry->depth || entry->generation() != generation_) {\n        entry->key32 = key32;\n        entry->score = static_cast<std::int16_t>(std::clamp(score, -32767, 32767));\n        entry->rule50_clock = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(std::min<int>(halfmove_clock, 100));\n
